## Seeded round 5 2026-11-04 (Mark-authorized)
- [ ] [P1] [decomposed] MissionEditor load then save silently drops patrol routes, defend_until and directive — `scripts/tools/mission_editor.gd` `_on_load_pressed()` (line ~396) copies grid, objective, terrain, cells and spawns into editor fields but never reads `mission.enemy_patrol_routes`, `mission.defend_until` or `mission.directive`, and `_on_save_pressed()` (line ~466) builds a fresh `MissionData.new()` that never sets them, so opening any existing mission and pressing Save permanently erases data (repo scan: 13 of the missions/*.tres files carry `enemy_patrol_routes` with Vector2i waypoints and mission_57.tres carries `defend_until`; the existing 9 tests in tests/test_mission_editor.gd only round-trip walls, spawns and objective fields, never these). Fix: add editor fields `_defend_until: int = -1`, `_directive: int = 0` and `_patrol_by_cell: Dictionary = {}` (enemy spawn cell to route Array); `_on_load_pressed` fills them (`_patrol_by_cell[mission.enemy_spawns[i]] = mission.patrol_route_for_index(i)` for non-empty routes); `_on_save_pressed` writes `mission.defend_until`, `mission.directive` and builds `mission.enemy_patrol_routes` in `_enemy_spawn_order` order using `_patrol_by_cell.get(cell, [])` (an erased or moved spawn naturally loses its route); `_on_new_pressed` resets all three. Do not touch battle.gd or mission_select.gd. Tests in tests/test_mission_editor.gd (use the existing `_save_and_reload()` helper): load mission_57 through `editor._load_option` selection then save and assert `defend_until` equals the original; load a mission whose `enemy_patrol_routes` is non-empty (find one by scanning missions/mission_*.tres at runtime) then save and assert the routes are equal; erasing that enemy spawn drops only its route; `_on_new_pressed` then save gives `defend_until == -1` and empty routes. VERIFY: `~/godot/godot4 --headless --path . -s addons/gut/gut_cmdln.gd -gtest=res://tests/test_mission_editor.gd -gexit` and confirm the raw Passing/Tests totals show 0 failing (GUT exits 0 even on failures; the new tests fail or error before the change).. {cat: game; size: S; multifile: no; research: none}
- [ ] [P2] [decomposed] MissionEditor dropdowns cannot represent Defend objective or the 9 non-wasteland/city themes — `scripts/tools/mission_editor.gd` `_OBJECTIVE_NAMES` (line 46) has 5 entries (Eliminate to Assassinate) but `MissionData.objective_type` documents 6 (5 = DEFEND, used by missions/mission_57.tres), so `_objective_option.select(_objective_type)` in `_on_load_pressed` selects nothing for a Defend mission and the author cannot choose Defend at all; the theme dropdown (line ~323) only offers "wasteland" and "city" and `_on_load_pressed` does `_theme_option.select(0 if _terrain_theme == "wasteland" else 1)`, so loading a dock/farmland/frozen/industrial/military/prison/suburban/subway/toxic mission (all exist in missions/*.tres) displays "city" and re-selecting anything overwrites the real theme. Fix: append "Defend" to `_OBJECTIVE_NAMES`; add `const _THEMES := ["wasteland", "city", "industrial", "dock", "farmland", "frozen", "military", "prison", "suburban", "subway", "toxic"]`, populate `_theme_option` from it and select with `_THEMES.find(_terrain_theme)` (fall back to adding the unknown string as an extra item so it is never relabeled). Tests in tests/test_mission_editor.gd: `MissionEditor._OBJECTIVE_NAMES.size() == 6`; loading mission_57 leaves `editor._objective_option.selected == 5`; a runtime scan of every missions/mission_*.tres asserts each `terrain_theme` is in `MissionEditor._THEMES` (list offenders by file name); loading a frozen-theme mission shows the "frozen" item as selected. - [ ] [P3] [decomposed] SaveManager.save_save mutates the caller's Dictionary (adds save_version) — `scripts/save/save_manager.gd` `save_save()` does `data["save_version"] = SAVE_VERSION` on the dictionary it was handed (GDScript Dictionaries are references), which already forced `InputRemapper.save_bindings()` to pass `current_bindings.duplicate()` and is a latent pollution bug for every other caller (a caller that keeps using its dict, e.g. a settings or roster dict, now has a foreign key in it). Fix: at the top of `save_save` after the name check, `data = data.duplicate()` (shallow is enough for the top-level stamp) and keep the existing `if not data.has("save_version")` stamping, so the written file is unchanged while the caller's dict is not. - [ ] [P3] [decomposed] CodexManager accepts arbitrary category strings and persists phantom categories — `scripts/codex/codex_manager.gd` `mark_seen(category, key)` only rejects an empty key, so a typo'd category (the file's own comment says the CAT_* constants exist to stop exactly this) silently creates a new bucket, saves it, emits `entry_revealed`, and `_load()` re-imports any category found in a hand-edited save; `seen_count`/`has_seen` for the real categories never see it. Fix: add `const VALID_CATEGORIES: Array[String] = [CAT_ENEMY, CAT_SOLDIER, CAT_TECH, CAT_FACTION]`; `mark_seen` returns false (no save, no signal) when `not VALID_CATEGORIES.has(category)`; `_load()` skips categories not in the list. Tests in tests/test_codex_manager.gd and tests/test_codex_manager_load.gd: `mark_seen("enemy", "grunt")` (typo) is false, `seen_count("enemy")` is 0 and no `entry_revealed` signal fires (use `watch_signals`); a codex save containing `{"seen": {"bogus": ["x"], "enemies": ["grunt"]}}` loads only `enemies` (`has_seen(CodexManager.CAT_ENEMY, "grunt")` true, `seen_count("bogus") == 0`); existing tests stay green. ## Seeded round 6 2026-11-06 (Mark-authorized)
- [ ] [P2] [decomposed] `RosterManager.normalize_loaded_entries` duplicate-rename can itself create a duplicate name — `scripts/roster/roster_manager.gd` `normalize_loaded_entries` (line ~573) keeps a per-base-name counter and renames the Nth repeat to `"%s-%d" % [name, count]` without checking whether that name already exists elsewhere in the list, so loaded names `["A", "A", "A-2"]` become `["A", "A-2", "A-2"]` (a python port of the function reproduces it); `_find_entry`, `set_deployed`, `sync_from_battle` (keyed by `u.name`) and `recruit_captured` all key on the name, so the two "A-2" soldiers alias each other (one is silently updated/dropped when the other deploys or dies). Existing tests (tests/test_roster_normalize.gd, tests/test_roster_load_hardening.gd) only cover `["Vega","Vega","Vega"]`. Fix: first pass builds a `used` Dictionary of every sanitized name in the list; second pass, for each repeat of an already-seen name, loop `n` upward from the counter until `"%s-%d" % [name, n]` is not in `used`, assign it and add it to `used`. Add tests/test_roster_normalize_collision.gd (`extends GutTest`): `[{name:"A"},{name:"A"},{name:"A-2"}]` normalizes to exactly `["A","A-3","A-2"]`; the result names always form a set of the same size as the list (check with a Dictionary) for `["A","A","A","A-2","A-3"]`; the original `["Vega","Vega","Vega"]` still gives `Vega, Vega-2, Vega-3`; and `deployed` capping is unchanged. VERIFY: `~/godot/godot4 --headless --path . -s addons/gut/gut_cmdln.gd -gtest=res://tests/test_roster_normalize_collision.gd -gexit` and confirm 0 failing in the raw totals (the A/A/A-2 case fails before). {cat: game; size: S; multifile: no; research: none}
- [ ] [P2] [decomposed] Benched survivors are never re-deployed, so a won mission can leave ZERO deployed soldiers and the next mission spawns no squad — `RosterManager.sync_from_battle` (scripts/roster/roster_manager.gd) drops only the soldiers in `fallen_names` and keeps benched ones with `deployed=false`; if all (or the only) deployed soldiers fall in a victory while others are benched, `get_deployed_count() == 0`, and `battle.gd:_spawn_units` (line ~612-622) loops over `get_deployed_entries()` so zero player units spawn; the roster screen's Bench button can also leave 0 deployed with no warning (`scripts/mission/roster_view.gd` only warns about a missing Medic). Fix (no battle.gd edit): at the end of `sync_from_battle` call a new `_ensure_squad_deployed()` that, when `_entries` is non-empty and `get_deployed_count() == 0`, marks the first `MAX_SQUAD` entries deployed (then `_save_roster()` once); and in roster_view.gd add `static func empty_squad_warning(deployed: int, total: int) -> String` returning "No soldiers deployed - the next mission would start with no squad" only when `total > 0 and deployed == 0` (else ""), shown as an orange Label in `_refresh()` next to the no-medic warning. Tests in new tests/test_roster_empty_squad.gd: `RosterManager.reset_roster()` (2 default soldiers), `set_deployed` both to false, then `var none: Array[Unit] = []; RosterManager.sync_from_battle(none, [])` gives `get_deployed_count() == 2`; with 6 benched-or-deployed soldiers and all 4 deployed names in `fallen_names` the 2 survivors get deployed; a squad that already has 1 deployed soldier is left untouched (count stays 1); an empty roster stays empty without error; `RosterView.empty_squad_warning(0, 3) != ""`, `(2, 3) == ""`, `(0, 0) == ""`; call `RosterManager.reset_roster()` at the end of each test to restore state. VERIFY: `~/godot/godot4 --headless --path . -s addons/gut/gut_cmdln.gd -gtest=res://tests/test_roster_empty_squad.gd -gexit` and confirm 0 failing in the raw totals (the deployed==2 case fails before). {cat: game; size: M; multifile: yes; research: none}
- [ ] [P2] [decomposed] Destructive one-click buttons have no confirmation: "Reset Techs" and slot "Delete" — `scripts/mission/tech_tree.gd` `_on_reset_pressed()` calls `TechManager.reset_techs()` immediately (all research gone, credits/materials already spent are not refunded) and `scripts/mission/save_slot_select.gd` `_on_slot_deleted()` deletes the slot's roster/tech/settings/battle/codex saves on a single press, with the Delete button sitting next to Continue; grep of scripts/ for "confirm" finds no confirmation anywhere. ## Seeded round 8 2026-11-09 (Mark-authorized)
- [ ] [P2] [decomposed] BuildInfo.is_valid_version accepts a trailing newline, so a version of "1.2.3\n" is reported as valid - `scripts/release/build_info.gd` `is_valid_version` compiles `^\d+\.\d+\.\d+$` and returns `regex.search(v) != null`; PCRE `$` also matches just before a final newline, so `"1.2.3\n"` passes (confirmed by running it: `assert_false(BuildInfo.is_valid_version("1.2.3\n"))` fails) and would be written into build metadata, whereas `ReleaseCheck._is_valid_version` and `ItchReadinessCheck.validate_itch_channel_name` already guard this with `result.get_string() == v`. Fix: `var result: RegExMatch = regex.search(v)` then `return result != null and result.get_string() == v`. Extend tests/test_build_info.gd with `is_valid_version("1.2.3\n")` false, `"1.2.3"` true, `"10.20.30"` true, `"1.2"` false, `"1.2.3.4"` false, `"v1.2.3"` false, `""` false, `" 1.2.3"` false. VERIFY: `~/godot/godot4 --headless --path . -s addons/gut/gut_cmdln.gd -gtest=res://tests/test_build_info.gd -gexit` and confirm 0 failing in the raw totals (the trailing-newline case fails before). {cat: infra; size: S; multifile: no; research: none}
- [ ] [P3] [decomposed] Objective.progress_ratio for ELIMINATE is not clamped, so a bad context gives a negative or over-1.0 progress ratio - `scripts/mission/objective.gd` `progress_ratio` case 0 returns `float(enemies_total - enemies_alive) / float(enemies_total)` with no clamp (the SURVIVE and DEFEND cases already use `clampf`); with `enemies_total=2, enemies_alive=3` (reinforcements spawned after the total was captured) it returns -0.5 and with `enemies_alive=-1` it returns 1.5 (both confirmed by running), which any progress bar or `MissionHub` percent format would show as a negative or >100 percent. Fix: wrap in `clampf(..., 0.0, 1.0)`. Extend tests/test_objective_progress.gd (it uses `Objective.progress_ratio(0, {...})`): `{enemies_total: 2, enemies_alive: 3}` is 0.0, `{enemies_total: 2, enemies_alive: -1}` is 1.0, `{enemies_total: 4, enemies_alive: 1}` is 0.75 (assert_almost_eq 0.001), `{enemies_total: 0, enemies_alive: 5}` stays 1.0. VERIFY: `~/godot/godot4 --headless --path . -s addons/gut/gut_cmdln.gd -gtest=res://tests/test_objective_progress.gd -gexit` and confirm 0 failing in the raw totals (the alive>total and alive<0 cases fail before). {cat: game; size: S; multifile: no; research: none}
- [ ] [P3] [decomposed] WeightedPick lets a negative weight corrupt the buckets so later entries can never be picked - `scripts/battle/weighted_pick.gd` `cumulative` and `index_for_roll` sum weights as-is; for weights `[5, -3, 2]` the running totals are 5, 2, 4 so every roll lands in index 0 (index 2 is unreachable; confirmed by running 4 rolls, all returned 0), and tests/test_weighted_pick.gd only covers zero weights. Fix: treat any weight below 0 as 0 in both functions (`var wt: int = maxi(int(w), 0)` inside each loop). Extend tests/test_weighted_pick.gd (it preloads the script as `WeightedPick`): `cumulative([5, -3, 2]) == [5, 5, 7]`; `index_for_roll([5, -3, 2], r)` is 0 for r in 0..4 and 2 for r in 5 and 6, never 1; `index_for_roll([-1, -2], 0) == -1`; `index_for_roll([3, 0, 1], 3) == 2` still holds. VERIFY: `~/godot/godot4 --headless --path . -s addons/gut/gut_cmdln.gd -gtest=res://tests/test_weighted_pick.gd -gexit` and confirm 0 failing in the raw totals (the negative-weight cases fail before). {cat: game; size: S; multifile: no; research: none}
- [ ] [P3] [decomposed] TurnSnapshot.capture crashes on a non-Dictionary entry instead of skipping it - `scripts/turn/turn_snapshot.gd` `capture` does `(s as Dictionary).duplicate(true)` for every element; `capture([{"a": 1}, 5])` raises a runtime error on the int and returns an incomplete array (confirmed by running: size was not 1 after the error), and `restore` delegates to it, so one corrupt saved entry breaks an undo/restore. Keep the file's 4-space indentation. Fix: inside the loop `if typeof(s) != TYPE_DICTIONARY: continue` before the duplicate. Extend tests/test_turn_snapshot_edges.gd (tab-indented): `capture([{"a": 1}, 5, null, "x", {"b": 2}])` has size 2, `snap[0]["a"] == 1`, `snap[1]["b"] == 2`; `restore` of that result has the same size; and the result entries are deep copies (mutating the source `{"b": 2}` afterwards does not change `snap[1]`). VERIFY: `~/godot/godot4 --headless --path . -s addons/gut/gut_cmdln.gd -gtest=res://tests/test_turn_snapshot_edges.gd -gexit` and confirm 0 failing in the raw totals (the mixed-entry case fails before). {cat: game; size: S; multifile: no; research: none}
- [ ] [P3] [decomposed] ItchUploader.build_butler_command builds a butler push argv with an empty build path - `scripts/release/itch_uploader.gd` `build_butler_command(build_path, channel)` validates only the channel; `build_butler_command("", "windows")` returns a 4-element argv with an empty third argument (confirmed by running: size 4, expected 0), so `butler push "" game:windows` would run against the working directory or fail obscurely. Fix: after the channel check add `if build_path.strip_edges().is_empty(): push_error("Empty itch build path"); return PackedStringArray()`. Extend tests/test_itch_uploader.gd (push_error on stderr is expected there, see the invalid-channel test): `build_butler_command("", "windows").size() == 0`, `("   ", "windows").size() == 0`, and `("build/xlite.zip", "windows").size() == 4` still. VERIFY: `~/godot/godot4 --headless --path . -s addons/gut/gut_cmdln.gd -gtest=res://tests/test_itch_uploader.gd -gexit` and confirm 0 failing in the raw totals (the empty-path cases fail before). {cat: infra; size: S; multifile: no; research: none}
- [ ] [P2] [decomposed] StatusModifiers.get_move_speed_multiplier is order-dependent and ignores slowed, disagreeing with move_multiplier_percent - `scripts/units/status_modifiers.gd` `get_move_speed_multiplier(effects)` returns 0.5 as soon as it sees "blind" and only returns 0.0 if "stunned" comes earlier, so `["blind", "stunned"]` gives 0.5 (confirmed by running; a stunned unit must be 0.0), and it never handles "slowed" (`["slowed"]` gives 1.0 while `move_multiplier_percent(["slowed"])` is 50); the function has no test (tests/test_status_modifiers.gd only calls `move_multiplier_percent` and `aim_penalty`). Fix: make it `return float(move_multiplier_percent(effects)) / 100.0` so there is one source of truth. Extend tests/test_status_modifiers.gd with `assert_almost_eq(StatusModifiers.get_move_speed_multiplier(X), Y, 0.001)` for `["blind", "stunned"]` -> 0.0, `["stunned", "blind"]` -> 0.0, `["slowed"]` -> 0.5, `["blind"]` -> 0.5, `["slowed", "blind"]` -> 0.5, `[]` -> 1.0, `["suppressed"]` -> 1.0. VERIFY: `~/godot/godot4 --headless --path . -s addons/gut/gut_cmdln.gd -gtest=res://tests/test_status_modifiers.gd -gexit` and confirm 0 failing in the raw totals (the blind+stunned and slowed cases fail before). {cat: game; size: S; multifile: no; research: none}
- [x] [P3] [decomposed] SpiralCells.count_in_radius(-1) returns 1 while filled_area(center, -1) is empty - `scripts/grid/spiral_cells.gd` `count_in_radius` computes `side = 2 * radius + 1` and returns `side * side`, so radius -1 gives 1 (confirmed by running; expected 0) and radius -2 gives 9, but `filled_area` for any negative radius returns an empty array (pinned by tests/test_spiral_cells_filled_area.gd `test_negative_radius_is_empty`), so the count and the area disagree. ## Seeded round 9 2026-11-10 (Mark-authorized, addendum)
- [ ] [P3] [decomposed] CameraInput.remap_touch_to_camera passes NaN straight through, so a degenerate pinch (two touches at the same point gives 0/0) sets the camera zoom to NaN and the pan to NaN - `scripts/battle/camera_input.gd` `remap_touch_to_camera(delta, pinch_scale)` clamps with `clampf`, which does not clamp NaN (confirmed by running it: `remap_touch_to_camera(Vector2(3, 4), NAN)` returns zoom nan, and `Vector2(NAN, 4)` returns pan (nan, 4)); infinity is already clamped to the bounds. Fix with explicit types and no Variant inference: `var dx: float = 0.0 if is_nan(delta.x) else delta.x`, `var dy: float = 0.0 if is_nan(delta.y) else delta.y`, `var scale: float = 1.0 if is_nan(pinch_scale) else pinch_scale`, then clamp `dx`, `dy` and `scale * PINCH_ZOOM_SENSITIVITY` exactly as before (a draft of this change passes the 5 existing tests and the new one). Extend tests/test_camera_input.gd with `test_nan_inputs_are_neutral`: `var r: Dictionary = CameraInput.remap_touch_to_camera(Vector2(NAN, 4.0), NAN)`, `assert_eq(r["zoom"], 1.0)`, `assert_eq(r["pan"], Vector2(0.0, 4.0))`; keep the five existing tests. VERIFY: `~/godot/godot4 --headless --path . -s addons/gut/gut_cmdln.gd -gtest=res://tests/test_camera_input.gd -gexit` and confirm 0 failing in the raw totals (the NaN test fails before). {cat: game; size: S; multifile: no; research: none}
